From two iterations onward, the measured LER remains below the physical error probability for every evaluated value of p. For one to five iterations, the maximum sampled serialized X+Z DPU compute latency remains below the 1 ms decoder-side reference for trapped-ion QEC, reaching approximately 0.873 ms at five iterations. These results show that near-memory processing can provide high aggregate throughput and sub-millisecond compute latency for qLDPC BP decoding under the evaluated conditions.

</details>


### [116] [Pulsed single-photon magnetometry with a $Λ$-type three-level system: near-optimal frequency-resolved photon counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27979)
*Seyed Mostafa Moniri,Elnaz Darsheshdar,Mikayel Khanbekyan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了在Λ型三能级系统中脉冲单光子磁力测量，通过分解量子费舍尔信息探讨环境耦合对磁场信息提取的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在存在环境耦合的情况下提高单光子磁力测量的精度。

Method: 利用受经典控制场驱动的Λ型三能级系统，推导渐近输出态并分解其量子费舍尔信息。

Result: 发现环境耦合可重塑散射响应并增加磁场信息，在临界耦合时频率零点使频谱强度携带几乎全部磁场信息。

Conclusion: 对于长高斯脉冲，额外的频谱强度贡献和残余相位信息差距随时间平方或更快下降。

Abstract: We investigate pulsed single-photon magnetometry with a Zeeman-sensitive $Λ$-type three-level system driven by a classical control field. We derive the asymptotic output state and decompose its quantum Fisher information into photon-loss, spectral-intensity, and spectral-phase contributions. Environmental coupling reshapes the scattering response and can increase magnetic-field information. At critical coupling, real-frequency zeros of the scattering amplitude redistribute information toward measurable spectral intensity, allowing frequency-resolved photon counting to capture nearly all of the magnetic-field information encoded in the output state when the zeros lie within the pulse bandwidth. For long Gaussian pulses with a smooth, nonzero central-frequency scattering amplitude, the additional spectral-intensity contribution and residual spectral-phase information gap decrease as $T^{-2}$ or faster.

</details>


### [117] [Predicting Multipartite Entanglement in Quantum Circuits using Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27993)
*Darell Timothy Tarigan,Fadhil Fatih Shiddiq,Hadyan Luthfan Prihadi,Donny Dwiputra,Jusak S. Kosasih,Yanoar P. Sarwono,Freddy Permana Zen,Rui-Qin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图的Transformer代理模型QIG-Fusion，用于高效预测参数化量子电路中的一阶和二阶纠缠度量，显著降低了量子架构搜索中的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒙特卡洛采样在测量多部分纠缠时随系统规模扩大而计算代价高昂，难以适用于大规模参数化量子电路的纠缠分析。

Method: 引入qubit-interconnected graph (QIG)编码，将每个量子比特表示为节点，通过加权邻接记录纠缠门的出现次数，并融合门级DAG编码器构建QIG-Fusion模型，以预测Meyer-Wallach（Q1）和Scott（Q2）纠缠度量。

Result: 在4到8量子比特、共50,000个电路上的十次种子实验中，QIG-Fusion对Q1和Q2的预测RMSE分别低至0.038和0.037，Spearman秩相关系数高达0.95。

Conclusion: QIG-Fusion能够高效且准确地估计参数化量子电路的多部分纠缠，解决了传统方法计算成本高的问题，为大规模量子架构搜索提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Multipartite entanglement is a critical property of parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs), particularly for near-term hybrid quantum-classical algorithms, as it characterizes their ability to generate highly entangled states. However, measuring entanglement remains computationally expensive because conventional Monte Carlo sampling scales unfavorably with system size. To overcome this challenge, we introduce a graph-based transformer surrogate that predicts both the first-order Meyer-Wallach measure ($Q_1$) and the second-order Scott measure ($Q_2$), resolving entanglement structures indistinguishable under $Q_1$ alone. Our central contribution is the qubit-interconnected graph (QIG) encoding for transformers, where each node represents a qubit and weighted adjacencies record entangling-gate multiplicities. Fused with a gate-level DAG encoder, this yields the QIG-Fusion model. Evaluated on 50,000 circuits spanning 4- to 8-qubit systems across a ten-seed protocol, QIG-Fusion achieves an RMSE as low as 0.037 ($Q_2$) and 0.038 ($Q_1$), with a Spearman rank correlation up to 0.95. This framework significantly reduces the computational cost of Quantum Architecture Search (QAS), enabling efficient entanglement estimation for large-scale PQCs.

</details>


### [118] [Physics-Constrained Conditional Generative Learning for Quantum State and Process Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28035)
*Daming Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理约束的条件生成对抗网络，用于量子态和过程层析成像，通过可微Cholesky层保证密度矩阵的物理合法性，并揭示了L1惩罚项对GHZ相干性出现的关键影响，框架可扩展至多量子比特系统并结合张量网络以提升可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的量子态与过程层析技术在多量子比特系统中面临计算开销过大的问题，亟需一种高效、可扩展的数据驱动方法来实现对NISQ设备的有效诊断。

Method: 引入一种物理约束的条件生成对抗网络（GAN），直接学习从Pauli期望值到密度矩阵的前向生成映射；生成器输出端嵌入可微Cholesky层，以自动满足Hermiticity、正定性和单位迹的物理约束；并将该结构推广至Choi矩阵表示以实现量子过程层析。

Result: 实验表明L1惩罚项的强度显著影响GHZ态相干性的出现：适中的惩罚值可实现最快且稳定的收敛；对于高温热态，需要过完备测量基以抑制后期波动；在6个及以上量子比特系统中，密度矩阵维度指数增长仍是瓶颈，但可通过引入张量网络结构控制迭代成本并保持重构保真度。

Conclusion: 物理约束的生成学习为噪声中等规模量子设备提供了一条可扩展且可摊销的数据驱动层析路径。

Abstract: Quantum state and process tomography constitute essential diagnostic tools in quantum information science, yet their standard formulations suffer from prohibitive computational scaling as the number of qubits grows. In this work, we introduce a physics-constrained conditional generative adversarial network that bypasses iterative constrained inversion by directly learning a forward generative mapping conditioned on Pauli expectation values. The generator embeds a differentiable Cholesky layer at its output, which enforces Hermiticity, positive semidefiniteness, and unit trace by construction. Our experiments reveal that the strength of the $L^1$ penalty critically governs the emergence of GHZ coherence during training: an excessively large penalty postpones the coherence onset and yields a prolonged low-fidelity plateau, whereas an intermediate value enables the fastest stable convergence. Moreover, for high-temperature thermal states, an over-complete measurement basis proves necessary to prevent sustained late-stage fluctuations. By extending the same Cholesky constraint to the Choi-matrix representation, the framework naturally accommodates quantum process tomography. For systems with $n \ge 6$ qubits, the exponential growth of the underlying $2^n \times 2^n$ density matrix remains the fundamental bottleneck; we discuss how integrating tensor-network structures can contain the per-iteration cost while preserving reconstruction fidelity. Altogether, these results suggest that physically constrained generative learning offers a scalable and amortizable pathway toward data-driven tomography for noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices.

</details>


### [119] [Engineering Coherence and Ergotropy through Spatial Arrangement of Environmental Channel in a Two-Qubit Quantum Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28153)
*Gayatree Swain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了在XY交换相互作用下，两个共振量子比特量子电池中环境通道的空间排列对相干性和功提取动力学的影响，发现不同的空间构型和初始相干分布可调节局部和集体可提取功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索环境通道的空间安排如何影响开放量子电池中的相干性保持与功提取效率。

Method: 采用局部l1范数相干性、全局ergotropy及全局-局域ergotropy差距作为评估指标，分析不同构型（分离与共位）和初始态下的动力学行为。

Result: 较强的交换相互作用促进相干性再分配但不提升可提取功的保持；共位构型保留更多全局ergotropy，尤其当受RTN噪声影响较小时；该构型也产生更大的ergotropy差距，且差距随耦合强度和热化程度变化。

Conclusion: 总可提取功、局部相干性和集体功优势反映了量子电池性能的不同方面，初始相干分布与空间结构是调控可提取功的有效机制。

Abstract: We investigate how the spatial arrangement of environment channel influences the dynamics of coherence and work extraction in a two-qubit quantum battery with resonant qubits coupled through an XY-exchange interaction. Qubit B is attached to a finite-temperature thermal bath, while RTN noise is applied either to qubit A or to qubit B, depending on whether it is in a separated or co-located configuration, respectively. Two complementary product states are considered to initialise the coherence on different qubits. The dynamics are evaluated using local $l_{1}$-norm coherence, global ergotropy and the global-local ergotropy gap. The results suggest that a stronger exchange interaction enhances the redistribution of coherence without necessarily improving retention of extractable work. For the parameter investigated, the greater global ergotropy is retained in the case of the co-located configuration, especially when the initially coherent qubit is not directly affected by the RTN. However, the configuration provides the largest global-local ergotropy gap, which varies with the exchange interaction, initial state and thermal coupling. Suppression of the gap at large $J$ is typically observed in the case of stronger thermalisation, although enhancement of intermediate-coupling may occur. These results establish that total extractable work, local coherence and collective work advantage quantify the distinct aspects of quantum battery performances. The initial distribution of coherence and the spatial arrangement thus provide complementary mechanisms for regulating locally and collectively extractable work in interacting open quantum batteries.

</details>


### [120] [A quantum generative model for in silico clinical trials using scarce training datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28168)
*Olatz Sanz Larrarte,Reza Dastbasteh,Roberto Sanchez-Navarro,Maria Diez-Campelo,Felipe Prosper,Ana Alfonso-Pierola,Mikel Hernaez,Sara Capponi,Pedro Crespo Bofill,Josu Etxezarreta Martinez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于量子计算的生成模型，用于生成高保真度的虚拟患者数据，尤其适用于样本稀缺的罕见病临床试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成模型在训练样本稀少时表现不佳，而临床试验中尤其是罕见病数据获取困难，需要更有效的生成方法。

Method: 利用量子计算机表示复杂概率分布的能力，构建能够融合非对称数据库的量子电路作为生成模型，并在IBM Heron量子计算机上实现。

Result: 在骨髓增生异常综合征（MDS）患者的7个临床变量数据集上验证了该方法的有效性，结果显示量子生成模型在泛化性和表达能力上优于经典生成模型。

Conclusion: 量子生成模型在生成高保真虚拟患者方面具有潜力，可作为临床试验中补充真实数据的有效工具。

Abstract: In silico methods have emerged as a strategy to complement clinical trials. These are particularly relevant for rare or heterogeneous diseases for which traditional methods are costly or difficult to apply. While classical generative models have shown an extremely good ability to generate high fidelity data when trained using extensive databases, they often struggle when the available samples for training are scarce. In this work, we leverage the potential of quantum computers to represent complex probability distributions to generate high fidelity in silico patients. We propose a pipeline able to combine asymmetric databases into a quantum circuit that serves as a quantum generative model. We evaluate the efficacy of our proposal using a database of Myelodysplastic Syndrome (MDS) patients with 7 clinical variables as a proof-of-concept. We executed our quantum generative model in the IBM Heron r2 ``ibm\_basquecountry'' superconducting quantum computer and compare our method with well known classical baselines. Our results show that the quantum generative model surpasses the classical generative models in generalization and expressivity metrics, indicating its potential validity to generate high fidelity in silico patients for clinical trials.

</details>


### [121] [Postselection-loophole-free Bell test under strict spacetime constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28190)
*Kannan Vijayadharan,Matías Rubén Bolaños,Andrea Pompermaier,Tommaso Bertapelle,Francesco B. L. Santagiustina,Costantino Agnesi,Giuseppe Vallone,Paolo Villoresi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究展示了一种基于光纤的时间-比特纠缠的贝尔测试平台，同时关闭了局域性、自由选择和后选择漏洞，实现了超过265倍标准差的CHSH不等式违背，验证了非定域性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间-比特纠缠在光纤中分发光子纠缠应用广泛，但传统的干涉测量引入了后选择漏洞，使得无漏洞贝尔测试难以实现。因此需要一种能够同时关闭多个实验漏洞的新型平台。

Method: 构建了一个基于光纤的时间-比特纠缠贝尔测试系统，采用主动切换和高速调制技术，避免后选择问题，并确保空间分离和测量独立性，从而同时关闭局域性、自由选择和后选择漏洞。

Result: 观测到CHSH值为S=2.583±0.002，超过定域实在论界限265个标准差，且实验距离仅为49.0±0.7米，显著短于以往类似约束下的光子贝尔实验。

Conclusion: 该工作成功实现了严格无漏洞的贝尔不等式检验，证明时间-比特纠缠可用于实用化的设备无关量子通信和未来量子互联网。

Abstract: Entanglement gives rise to correlations between distant quantum systems that cannot be explained by local realistic theories. Bell inequality violations provide a direct way to reveal these correlations and certify nonlocality, especially when the relevant experimental loopholes are closed. Time-bin encoding, in which quantum information is encoded into well-defined temporal modes, is a commonly used platform for distributing photonic entanglement in optical fibers. Yet loophole-free Bell tests with time-bin entanglement have received comparatively little attention, owing in part to the postselection loophole introduced by conventional interferometric measurements. Here, we demonstrate a fiber-based platform for Bell tests with time-bin entanglement that simultaneously closes the locality, freedom-of-choice, and postselection loopholes. We observe a CHSH violation of $S=2.583 \pm 0.002$, exceeding the local-realistic bound by over 265 standard deviations. Notably, this rigorous certification of nonlocality is achieved at a separation distance of $49.0 \pm 0.7$ m, substantially shorter than previous photonic Bell tests addressing comparable space-time constraints. Beyond its foundational significance, our results demonstrate time-bin entanglement as a viable route towards practical device-independent quantum communication and a future quantum internet.

</details>


### [122] [Direct Adaptive Certification of High-Dimensional Entanglement with Bell Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28235)
*Xu Kang Tan,Jesvita Menezes,Sanjan D. Murthy,Adetunmise C. Dada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CGLMP不等式和复杂同时扰动随机逼近（CSPSA）的自适应贝尔测试方法，用于在无需重构密度矩阵的情况下高效检测高维未知纠缠态中的非局域关联，并在数值上验证了其在维度d=2到8范围内的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维纠缠态具有更丰富的量子资源，但其表征因测量自由度增加而变得困难；现有自适应贝尔测试方法多集中于比特系统，缺乏对高维系统的有效扩展。

Method: 将CSPSA优化算法扩展至基于CGLMP不等式的高维贝尔测试，通过自适应优化测量设置来最大化贝尔不等式违背值，从而检测未知量子态中的非局域性，且无需量子态层析。

Result: 在d=2到8的有限采样模拟中均实现了可信的CGLMP不等式违背；对于可见度仅高于阈值0.05的各向同性混合态，也能在整个维度范围内实现认证违背；与量子态层析相比，CSPSA在d≥6时使用的测量配置更少，而层析在d≤8时每项认证所需探测粒子对更少。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一条极具吸引力的路径用于高维纠缠的认证，因其每次优化迭代的测量代价与维度无关，适合扩展至更高维度系统。

Abstract: Entangled photons play a crucial role in quantum applications, and determining and characterising their entanglement is vital to using them effectively. High-dimensional entangled states offer richer possibilities, but their additional measurement degrees of freedom make them increasingly demanding to characterise. However, adaptive Bell-test methods based on complex simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (CSPSA) have so far focused mainly on qubits. Here we numerically investigate a Bell-inequality-violation-based method for detecting entanglement in unknown quantum states. We extend CSPSA to high-dimensional Bell testing by using the Collins-Gisin-Linden-Massar-Popescu (CGLMP) inequality for bipartite qudits. The resulting protocol can detect Bell-nonlocal correlations in unknown entangled states, whether pure or mixed, without first reconstructing their density matrix. Using 100 optimisation iterations in each of 100 independent finite-shot runs per number of dimensions d, we demonstrate certified CGLMP violations throughout d=2-8. For isotropic mixed states tested at a visibility of just 0.05 above the standard-Fourier violation threshold, we likewise observe confidence-certified CGLMP violations throughout the range of d studied. We compare this direct stochastic approach with quantum state tomography, the standard method for characterising an unknown state. In the matched benchmark, CSPSA uses fewer measurement configurations per attempt from d=6, whereas tomography requires fewer detected pairs per certified result through d=8. We also derive the phase dependence of the CGLMP parameter and clarify the features of its landscape that govern the adaptive search. Because the measurement-setting cost of each CSPSA iteration is independent of dimension, the method offers a particularly attractive route to the certification of high-dimensional entanglement.

</details>


### [123] [Role of the Drive in Mediating Correlations Between Two Qubits Through a Shared Dissipative Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28251)
*Nozhat Ghaseminezhad,Vahid Ameri,Alidad Askari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用精确的主方程表明，两个仅通过共享阻尼驱动腔耦合的量子比特可以产生关联，不同驱动方式影响关联类型：参数化驱动可生成纠缠，而其他驱动仅产生量子失谐；温度升高会破坏纠缠但增强失谐，揭示了从纠缠到失谐的温度驱动转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在共享耗散环境中量子比特间如何产生量子关联，并区分纠缠与量子失谐的行为。

Method: 使用数值精确的主方程模拟，并结合绝热消除模型分析有效耦合和集体去相位通道的作用。

Result: 发现非调制驱动下仅出现随温度增加的量子失谐而无纠缠；只有时间调制的参数驱动能产生显著纠缠（对数负性达0.32）；升温导致纠缠消失但失谐持续增长；参数驱动最有利于保持单量子比特相干性。

Conclusion: 腔体诱导的有效耦合和集体去相位通道解释了失谐与纠缠的不同行为，展示了通过驱动方式和温度调控量子关联类型的可能路径。

Abstract: Using a numerically exact master equation, we demonstrate that two qubits, coupled solely through a shared damped, driven cavity, can become correlated. The drive influences both the amount and the type of correlation. For parametric, coherent, and resonantly modulated drives, the qubits develop quantum discord that increases with cavity temperature, while the logarithmic negativity remains numerically zero. This indicates the presence of discord without entanglement. In contrast, a time-modulated parametric drive is the only one that generates genuine two-qubit entanglement, achieving \(E_\mathcal{N} \simeq 0.15\) and concurrence \(\simeq 0.16\) at \((\eps, γ) = (0.3, 0.2)\), which rises to \(E_\mathcal{N} \simeq 0.32\) in the weak-coupling, moderate-damping region. Heating eventually destroys this entanglement around \(n_{\mathrm{th}} \simeq 0.2\), while discord continues to grow, resulting in a temperature-driven transition from entanglement to discord within a single drive. Moreover, the parametric drive offers the best protection for single-qubit coherence, unlike the coherent and modulated drives. An adiabatic-elimination model indicates that the cavity generates an effective coupling and a collective dephasing channel, both of which increase with temperature, explaining the observed discord without entanglement.

</details>


### [124] [Quantum Federated Learning Based on Bures--Uhlmann Geometry for Heterogeneous Noisy Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28379)
*Haruki Emori,Masaki Uchihara,Yuuki Tokunaga*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合态几何张量的量子联邦学习方法，利用Bures度量和平均Uhlmann曲率来应对噪声量子设备中的参数不兼容性和客户端异质性，提升了模型在高噪声环境下的准确性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子联邦学习面临数据和硬件异质性问题，尤其是噪声量子设备导致的参数不兼容性，现有纯态方法和对角近似忽略了关键的相关性信息。

Method: 将参数空间几何扩展到混合态，使用混合态几何张量的实部（Bures度量）作为局部预条件器，虚部（平均Uhlmann曲率）设计动态聚合规则以降低不可靠客户端的权重。

Result: 在离子阱量子模拟器上的实验表明，该方法在多种设备异质条件下保持高精度，优于标准联邦平均算法，后者在强噪声下性能下降明显；并证明了收敛性和方差优势的理论保证。

Conclusion: 通过引入混合态几何结构，有效解决了噪声环境下量子联邦学习中的参数不兼容与客户端不可靠问题，为实用化量子联邦学习提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning enables collaborative model training across quantum devices without sharing raw data, and it faces the data and hardware heterogeneity inherent to noisy quantum devices. Utilizing the quantum geometric tensor is a natural remedy, yet pure-state approaches and diagonal approximations discard the correlations that encode parameter incompatibility. To address this, we extend the parameter-space geometry to the mixed states that noisy clients actually prepare. The real part of the resulting mixed-state geometric tensor is the Bures metric, which measures how fast the physical state changes under parameter variation, and the imaginary part is the mean Uhlmann curvature, which quantifies the incompatibility of estimating multiple parameters simultaneously. Accordingly, we employ the Bures metric as a local preconditioner and use the mean Uhlmann curvature to develop an achievable-precision aggregation rule that dynamically down-weights unreliable clients. Furthermore, we establish theoretical guarantees by proving a convergence theorem and a variance-dominance proposition. Empirical evaluations on a trapped-ion quantum emulator demonstrate that the proposed method maintains high accuracy across diverse device-heterogeneity conditions and outperforms standard federated averaging, whose accuracy degrades under strong noise.

</details>


### [125] [A Single Spin Switches the Steady-State Phase of an Open Quantum System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28390)
*Jianwen Jie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在耗散集体自旋系统中，增加一个自旋如何引发稳态相变。结果表明，在临界耗散失衡之上，单个自旋的加入会显著改变系统的宏观行为，导致从相平均环到南极固定点的转变，揭示了跃迁算符零点对稳态相的单组分控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多体系统中单个组分变化是否能引发宏观可观测量的显著变化，挑战传统认为单一组分改变仅产生微小影响的观点。

Method: 通过分析耗散集体自旋模型中的Dicke ladder结构，研究整数与半整数自旋梯级在非线性损耗幅值零点处的不同响应，并利用精确的有限尺寸稳态解揭示微观连接性差异导致的指数级权重竞争。

Result: 发现整数自旋梯级会采样损耗幅值的零点，使m<0的Dicke态为瞬态；而半整数梯级则避开该零点并保持单一常返类；这种差异在奇数N序列中引发一阶耗散相变，且单自旋加载实验证实无需后选择即可由稀有量子涨落触发状态切换。

Conclusion: 跃迁算符的零点提供了一种通过单个组分控制稳态相的新机制，实现了在多体耗散系统中的一阶相变调控。

Abstract: Changing a many-body system by one constituent is normally expected to produce only a vanishing correction to an intensive observable. Here we show that, above a critical dissipation imbalance, adding one spin at fixed intensive controls switches the steady state of a dissipative collective spin between a phase-averaged ring and a south-polar fixed point. Unlike previously proposed one-spin sensing through dark-state interference, parity here determines whether the Dicke ladder samples a zero of the state-dependent nonlinear-loss amplitude. The integer-spin ladder samples this zero, making Dicke states with m<0 transient; the half-integer ladder misses it and remains a single recurrent class. Exact finite-size steady states show that exponential competition between stationary weights amplifies this microscopic connectivity difference, yielding a first-order dissipative phase transition confined to the odd-N sequence. One-spin loading, with all populated spin sectors retained, confirms that rare quantum fluctuations induce the switch without postselection. Our results establish jump-operator zeros as a route for single-constituent control of steady-state phases.

</details>


### [126] [Energy Internet Routing using Quantum Optimization Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28401)
*Alireza Alamgir Tehrani,Mehrdad Boroushaki,Abbas Rajabi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种用于能源互联网中能量路由的QUBO和Ising哈密顿量新模型，并通过量子启发退火与量子门优化方法在9节点和30节点网络中验证了其有效性和可扩展性，结果显示经典方法运行时间更短，但模拟退火采样器在量子方法中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决能源互联网中可再生能源高效集成的问题，特别是能量路由这一NP难问题，需要开发新的优化模型以提升能效路径选择的性能。

Method: 提出了基于QUBO和Ising哈密顿量的能量路由建模方法，并采用量子启发退火、D-Wave系列采样器、QAOA及经典Dijkstra算法等多种优化技术进行求解与比较。

Result: 仿真结果表明所提模型能够准确匹配精确解；虽然经典方法总体运行时间优于量子方法，但在量子类方法中，模拟退火采样器具有最短运行时间。

Conclusion: 该QUBO与Ising建模框架为能源互联网中的能量路由提供了可行的量子计算解决方案，尽管当前量子方法在速度上尚不及经典算法，但展示了未来在可扩展性方面的潜力。

Abstract: The Energy Internet (EI) is a new concept aimed at enhancing the integration of renewable energy sources with the energy grid. Energy-efficient path selection in EI is NP-hard. This research presents an innovative Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) and Ising Hamiltonian formulation for energy routing. The validation and scalability of the proposed formulation were evaluated by applying quantum-inspired annealing and quantum gate optimization to two case studies, a 9-node and a 30-node EI network. A comparative analysis was presented between classical optimization using the Dijkstra algorithm, optimization-based methods, and QAOA using the Qiskit Sampler Primitive, NumpyEigenSolver, and quantum-inspired annealing using the Ocean exact Solver, D-Wave Tabu Sampler, and D-Wave Simulated Annealing. Simulation results based on the proposed formulation agree with the exact solution, while the runtime of classical approaches is less than that of quantum approaches. However, the Simulated Annealing sampler offers the shortest runtime among all quantum methods.

</details>


### [127] [Optomechanical inertial reference for atom interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28418)
*A. Rajagopalan,K. Stolzenberg,D. Thomas,A. Herbst,S. Abend,E. M. Rasel,F. Guzmán,D. Schlippert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将光机械谐振器与原子干涉仪融合的混合惯性传感器，利用谐振器的测试质量作为干涉仪的参考镜，显著提升了振动噪声抑制能力，并在宽频带内实现了高灵敏度加速度测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原子干涉仪在实验室外应用受限于振动噪声，且常规减振手段依赖外部传感器或笨重的隔振装置，因此需要一种更紧凑、高效的噪声抑制方案。

Method: 将光机械谐振器的测试质量用作原子干涉仪的参考镜，构建一体化混合传感器，通过共振跟踪读出技术实现对振动相位噪声的实时补偿。

Result: 谐振器实现8.6×10⁻¹⁵ m/√Hz的位移灵敏度和1.1×10⁻⁶ m/s²/√Hz的最小加速度灵敏度，带宽覆盖亚赫兹至2.5 kHz；系统在环境条件下消除高达50×10⁻³ m/s²加速度的相位模糊，并达到干涉仪的技术噪声极限。

Conclusion: 该集成架构通过共用机械元件增强了传感器间的相关性，无需依赖未知传递函数的外部传感器，且读出方式不依赖具体机械设计，可推广至其他精密传感平台。

Abstract: Atom interferometers are among the most sensitive inertial sensors, yet deployment outside the laboratory is limited by vibration noise, conventionally mitigated by external sensors or bulky isolation. Here we demonstrate a hybrid inertial sensor which fuses an optomechanical resonator and an atom interferometer by exploiting the resonator's test mass as the interferometer's reference mirror. This allows for better correlation than with two separate sensors, whose unknown transfer function is replaced by the static response of one mechanical element. The resonator achieves a displacement sensitivity of $8.6\cdot 10^{-15}$ m/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ with suppressed 1/f noise and yields a minimum acceleration sensitivity of $1.1\cdot10^{-6}$ m/s$^2$/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ over a bandwidth extending from sub-Hertz to 2.5 kHz. Under ambient laboratory conditions the integrated system removes vibration-induced phase ambiguity for accelerations up to $50\cdot 10^{-3}$ m/s$^2$ and reaches the interferometer's technical noise limit, which a commercial force-balance accelerometer does not. Because the resonance-tracking readout is largely independent of the mechanical design, the architecture transfers directly to other precision sensing platforms.

</details>


### [128] [Kerr nonlinearity and three-wave mixing in superconducting resonators hosting Al-InAs weak links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28428)
*Vittorio Buccheri,Ivo P. C. Cools,Nermin Trnjanin,Ankit Khola,Oleg Shvetsov,Thomas Kanne,Jesper Nygård,Attila Geresdi,Simone Gasparinetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一种基于铝包裹的砷化铟纳米线超导谐振器，实现了磁通和栅极偏置调控下的可调非线性，实验验证了三波混频并抑制了四波相互作用引起的克尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在量子信息处理中实现高效的参数放大和连续变量量子计算，需要设计具有有限三次非线性但抑制四次非线性的谐振器，以避免不利的克尔效应。

Method: 采用半导体-超导体混合器件构建含有两个弱连接的超导谐振器，并通过调节磁通和栅极偏置来表征其克尔非线性，同时建立基于Andreev束缚态的有效模型解释实验结果。

Result: 实验表明克尔非线性可通过磁通或栅极偏置调至零，同时证实了该器件中存在非零的三次非线性并实现了三波混频。

Conclusion: 半导体-超导体混合器件是实现可调非线性超导电路的有前景平台，适用于参数放大、玻色模式量子控制及微波模式间相互作用工程。

Abstract: Nonlinear microwave resonators are a versatile tool in quantum information processing, enabling parametric amplification, continuous variable quantum computing, and engineered mode interactions. Many of these applications especially benefit from cubic nonlinearities enabling three-wave mixing; at the same time, they are limited by quartic nonlinearities giving rise to undesired Kerr effects. A recurrent challenge is therefore to engineer resonators with a finite cubic nonlinearity while suppressing quartic terms. Here, we investigate a superconducting resonator hosting two weak links fabricated from an aluminum-capped indium arsenide nanowire. We characterize the Kerr nonlinearity as a function of magnetic flux and gate bias, showing that it can be tuned to zero with either control parameter. Furthermore, we experimentally demonstrate three-wave mixing in a semiconductor-superconductor hybrid device, establishing nonzero cubic nonlinearity. An effective model based on Andreev bound states qualitatively captures the observed trends. Our results validate semiconductor-superconductor hybrid devices as a promising platform for tunable nonlinear superconducting circuits, with applications in parametric amplification, quantum control of bosonic modes, and engineering interactions between microwave modes.

</details>


### [129] [From quantum reservoirs to quantum extreme learning machines through a nearest-neighbor spin chain with tunable quantum memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28440)
*Carlos Ramon-Escandell,Arnau Riera,Marcin Płodzień*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子储层计算（QRC）与量子极限学习机（QELM）被视为两种不同的计算范式，本文指出它们实际上是同一架构在不同输入编码长度下的两个极限：单个量子比特重写对应QRC，全部重写对应QELM。通过调节编码长度和系统参数，发现任务对历史依赖程度决定了最优记忆结构，且最佳性能出现在混沌边缘的动力学区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将QRC与QELM统一到一个连续可调的框架中，揭示量子时间信息处理中显式记忆与递归记忆之间的权衡机制。

Method: 引入输入编码长度作为控制参数，在固定规模量子系统中调节每次更新的量子比特数，并结合贝叶斯优化调整哈密顿量和演化时间，探索不同记忆分配策略下的计算性能。

Result: 发现QRC和QELM是编码长度的两个极端情况，中间值可实现二者间的平滑过渡；对于需要长时记忆的任务，递归量子记忆至关重要，而对于短时依赖任务，无记忆的QELM即可胜任；所有任务的最佳性能均出现在混沌边缘。

Conclusion: 量子时间处理的关键在于动力学状态（如混沌边缘），而非网络连接方式；通过调节输入编码长度可在固定硬件上灵活切换计算模式，实现性能最优化。

Abstract: Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) processes temporal data by retaining a memory of past inputs in the recurrent state of a quantum system, whereas a Quantum Extreme-Learning Machine (QELM) discards that memory, resetting the system at every step so that only the most recent input shapes the response. The two are usually treated as separate computational paradigms. We show that they are the two limits of a single architecture, connected by the input-encoding length, that is, the number of qubits overwritten with fresh data at each step. When a single qubit is re-encoded the system operates as a standard QRC, when the whole register is re-encoded it operates as a QELM, and intermediate lengths interpolate between them. The overwritten qubits hold the recent past in an explicit register, while the remaining qubits are never reset and carry older inputs forward in their evolving quantum state, so the encoding length redistributes memory between explicit and recurrent storage at fixed system size. Tuning the reservoir Hamiltonian and the evolution time with Bayesian optimization at each encoding length, we find that recurrent quantum memory is essential when a task must reach far into the past, and dispensable when the relevant history is short, where the memoryless reset limit already suffices. For every task the best reservoirs operate at the edge of chaos, where they perform as well as a densely connected reservoir with random all-to-all couplings of the same size, indicating that what temporal processing requires is the dynamical regime rather than the connectivity.

</details>


### [130] [Constant-time equilibration of observables under rapid Lindbladian dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28451)
*Štěpán Šmíd,Richard Meister,Mario Berta,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了马尔可夫开放系统中可观测量特定的混合时间，发现对于准局域且快速混合的林德布拉德系统，几何局域可观测量的和在与系统大小无关的时间内达到平衡，从而加速了耗散性量子算法（如量子吉布斯采样）的运行时间。同时提出了一种量子启发的经典算法，其运行时间在系统大小上也是线性的，但依赖于维度和精度的对数幂次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统使用全局迹距离来衡量收敛可能在只关心物理可观测量时过于严格，因此需要引入可观测量特定的混合时间以更有效地评估收敛行为。

Method: 通过理论分析准局域、快速混合的林德布拉德动力学下局域可观测量的演化，并对比全局状态混合时间；同时设计并分析一种量子启发的经典算法用于估计相同物理量。

Result: 证明了几何局域可观测量的和在与系统大小无关的时间内达到平衡，显著快于全局状态的对数依赖混合时间；提出了运行时间为系统大小线性、但指数依赖于log(1/ε)^D的经典算法；小规模模拟验证了理论预测的有效性且无显著隐藏常数。

Conclusion: 引入的可观测量特定混合时间能更高效地描述实际物理问题中的收敛行为，显著降低量子算法运行时间，并启发了具有竞争力的经典替代方案。

Abstract: Markovian open-system dynamics have widespread applications throughout quantum information science, including algorithmic state preparation. Their convergence is commonly quantified using the worst case global trace distance between the evolving and stationary states. However, this criterion can be unnecessarily stringent when only physically relevant observables are of interest. Here we introduce and study observable-specific mixing times. We prove that, for quasi-local, rapidly mixing Lindbladians, sums of geometrically local observables equilibrate in a time independent of system size, in contrast to the logarithmic dependence of global state mixing. This separation reduces the runtime of dissipative quantum algorithms, including quantum Gibbs samplers, for estimating quantities such as the Gibbs state energy and local order parameters, yielding an overall scaling that is linear in system size. Complementing this quantum result, we develop a quantum-inspired classical algorithm for estimating the same quantities. Its runtime is likewise linear in system size, but scaling exponentially in $\mathcal{O}\big(\log(1/ε)^D\big)$, where $D$ denotes the spatial dimension of the lattice. We further analyse non-interacting Lindbladians over qudits, fermions, and bosons, demonstrating that locality of observables is not always necessary for a qualitatively faster mixing. Small-scale simulations of quantum Gibbs samplers reveal no large hidden constants in our asymptotic analysis and show that the theoretical predictions closely capture the finite-size dynamics.

</details>


### [131] [Fabrication-free assessment of microwave losses in germanium-based dielectrics and superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28463)
*Haoran Lu,Kushagra Aggarwal,Xiangqin Wang,Pauline Drexler,Daniel Tong,Maciej W. Olszewski,Anand Ithepalli,Lingda Kong,Debdeep Jena,Peter L. McMahon,David A. Muller,Dominique Bougeard,Valla Fatemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于倒装芯片的传感方案，用于测量量子技术相关材料的有效微波损耗，无需在待测材料上进行器件制造。利用该方法研究了应变工程Ge/SiGe量子阱异质结构的微波损耗，并评估了PtSiGe超导薄膜的适用性，发现其微波损耗较高；通过在Pt上涂覆Nb可显著降低损耗并提高临界温度。结果为平面Ge异质结构上的微波电路设计提供了指导，并展示了一种快速测试新型超导量子电路材料的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在不需对目标材料进行器件加工的情况下，准确评估适用于量子技术的材料（如Ge/SiGe异质结构和超导薄膜）的微波损耗，开发一种非侵入、高效的表征方法。

Method: 采用基于倒装芯片的传感方案，通过测量微波谐振器的品质因数来反推材料的介电损耗；结合不同金属涂层（Pt与Nb/Pt）的热反应制备超导薄膜，并分析其微波损耗与温度依赖性。

Result: 成功量化了Ge/SiGe异质结构中各层的微波损耗，发现PtSiGe薄膜具有高微波损耗，限制了其在高质量谐振器中的应用；使用Nb涂层后，微波损耗显著降低，临界温度提升近三倍；损耗的温度依赖性表明两种超导薄膜均存在能隙不均匀性。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了倒装芯片传感是一种有效的快速材料筛选工具，明确了某些材料在超导量子电路中的应用限制，并为基于Ge异质结构的微波器件设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We present a flip-chip-based sensing scheme to measure effective microwave losses associated with target materials for quantum technologies, without requiring any device fabrication on the material under test. Using this approach, we quantify the microwave losses of a strain-engineered Ge/SiGe quantum well heterostructure and investigate losses arising from its Ge substrate and intermediate layers. The quality factors of the fabricated microwave resonators agree with the losses of dielectric materials independently extracted from flip-chip sensing measurements. We further study the superconductor platinum silicon germanide (PtSiGe) prepared by thermal reaction with a deposited Pt film, finding high microwave losses that limit the suitability of the films studied here as the sole superconductor for high-quality resonator applications. By coating Pt with Nb prior to the reaction, we observe a substantial reduction in microwave loss and a nearly three-fold enhancement of the transport critical temperature. The temperature dependence of the microwave loss is consistent with gap inhomogeneity in both superconducting films. These results identify constraints on material choices, provide design guidance for microwave circuits on planar Ge heterostructures, and demonstrate a fast-turnaround testing method for new materials for superconducting quantum circuits.

</details>


### [132] [Input-Output Analysis of Quantum Dot SUPER Excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28470)
*Johannes Kerber,Christoph Hotter,Helmut Ritsch,Klaus Mølmer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出并分析了一种名为SUPER的双色脉冲激发方案，利用红失谐光实现接近100%的量子发射体激发，并通过全量子输入-输出模型揭示其非线性三光子拉曼机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解SUPER方案中反直觉的动力学机制，特别是在自由空间中使用相干光脉冲时如何实现高保真度的种群反转。

Method: 采用扩展的量子输入-输出理论结合累积量展开方法，结合相互作用绘景下的全量子计算，分析自由空间中两个相干输入脉冲与两能级系统相互作用的动力学。

Result: 揭示了SUPER机制具有非线性的三光子拉曼特性，导致一个模式净减少2个光子，另一模式增加1个光子；证实自由空间中需要较高的脉冲光子数才能实现高效激发。

Conclusion: SUPER方案在自由空间中可通过高光子数相干脉冲实现高效激发，其核心是非线性多光子过程，该结果为无腔量子控制提供了理论支持。

Abstract: The Swing-UP of the quantum EmitteR population (SUPER) two-color pulsed excitation scheme allows for robust and close to 100\% excitation of a two-level quantum emitter using only red detuned light. We analyze the underlying counterintuitive dynamics using a full quantum input-output description of the in- and outgoing light pulses for the case of two coherent free-space input pulses at realistic photon numbers. At the microscopic level, the SUPER mechanism exhibits its nonlinear three-photon Raman-type character, leading to a net photon-number change of $-2$ in one mode and $+1$ in the other, as was first guessed from cavity-enhanced model descriptions at low photon numbers. We confirm that in free space, a sufficiently high pulse photon number, much larger than one, is required to achieve high-fidelity inversion. To treat the large coherent-state amplitudes (photon numbers) relevant for SUPER, we extend the quantum input-output formalism to include a cumulant expansion approach. With an interaction-picture formulation, the few exchanged photons that govern the nontrivial dynamics enable direct full-quantum calculations in truncated Hilbert spaces, including treatments in a displacement frame and for initial Fock-state pulses.

</details>


### [133] [Approaching Resource-Theoretic Optimal Performance with Structured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28488)
*Lea Lautenbacher,Giovanni Spaventa,Susana F. Huelga,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在光异构化过程中，资源理论预测的热力学操作界限是否能通过显式系统-环境相互作用实现，发现环境记忆效应可突破马尔可夫限制，但接近理论极限依赖于微观耦合结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨资源理论所预测的物理过程效率极限能否被现实动力学逼近，特别是在光异构化这一具体分子反应中检验该问题。

Method: 构建了一个可调的微观模型，描述分子光开关与结构化振动环境的耦合，并在马尔可夫与非马尔可夫 regime 之间连续过渡，结合资源理论分析不同热操作下的反应产率。

Result: 资源理论预测马尔可夫热操作的产率严格低于一般热操作；环境记忆效应能够解除马尔可夫动力学的限制，扩展可实现的态变换范围。

Conclusion: 虽然环境记忆有助于提升变换能力并接近资源理论预言的极限，但能否真正逼近该极限取决于微观耦合结构对动力学路径的引导作用。

Abstract: Resource-theoretic approaches to thermodynamics provide powerful, model-independent bounds on the efficiency of physical processes, because they do not rely on microscopic details of the environment. Whether such bounds can be approached by realistic dynamics generated by explicit system-environment interactions remains an open question. Photoisomerization, a fundamental molecular photoreaction, offers a concrete setting to examine this issue. We introduce a tunable microscopic model of a molecular photoswitch coupled to a structured vibrational environment, which interpolates continuously between Markovian and non-Markovian regimes. Resource-theoretic analysis predicts in particular that Markovian Thermal Operations achieve strictly lower yields than general Thermal Operations. We show that environmental memory lifts dynamical restrictions associated with Markovian thermal evolutions, thereby enlarging the set of transformations accessible to the microscopic dynamics. Approaching the thermal operation bound, however, depends on the microscopic coupling structure that generates this memory and directs the resulting dynamics towards the target transformation.

</details>


### [134] [Classical Commitment over Quantum Channels with Limited Entanglement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28500)
*Remi A. Chou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了在量子信道上具有有限预共享纠缠的经典字符串承诺问题，确定了一类信道的非交互式协议承诺容量，并分析了交互式协议的容量上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在有限预共享纠缠条件下，经典字符串承诺在量子信道上的容量极限，填补该领域理论空白。

Method: 通过分析一类输入维度为d的量子信道，结合条件熵H(Z|F)与可用纠缠率E，推导非交互和交互协议的承诺容量。

Result: 对于非交互协议，承诺容量为min{H(Z|F), log₂d + E}；对于交互协议，承诺率不超过log₂d + E，当H(Z|F) ≥ log₂d + E时交互不提升容量，并确定了量子擦除信道在交互情况下的容量。

Conclusion: 该研究完全刻画了一类重要量子信道的字符串承诺容量，揭示了交互性在某些条件下对容量无增益的现象。

Abstract: We study classical string commitment over quantum channels with limited preshared entanglement. For noninteractive protocols, we determine the commitment capacity of a class of channels with input dimension $d$ that, at each use, sample a pair of classical random variables $(F,Z)$, apply one of the $d^2$ Heisenberg--Weyl operators indexed by $Z$ to the input, and deliver the transformed quantum system together with $F$ to the receiver. If $E$ is the available entanglement rate in bits per channel use, then the capacity is $\min\{H(Z|F),\log_2d+E\}$. This class of channels encompasses quantum erasure and depolarizing channels, as well as families of Pauli channels. Additionally, for interactive protocols, we show that the commitment rate cannot exceed $\log_2d+E$ bits per channel use, so that when $H(Z|F)\geq\log_2d+E$, interactive communication does not increase the capacity. As a consequence, for interactive protocols, we determine the capacity of the quantum erasure channel.

</details>


### [135] [Langevin Theory of Non-Markovian Quantum Dynamics: Application to Delayed Coherent Feedback and the Laser Linewidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28506)
*Marc Cuenca-Laràs,Ming Li,Carlos Navarrete-Benlloch,Germán J. de Valcárcel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相空间表示的非马尔可夫开放量子系统的一般朗之万框架，通过将环境自由度积分出去，实现了确定性记忆核与随机噪声的分离，并成功应用于延迟相干反馈下的激光线宽研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展相空间方法至非马尔可夫动力学长期存在挑战，本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一种能精确处理非马尔可夫效应且适用于强耦合和非线性系统的通用框架。

Method: 对完整的系统-环境（S+E）联合体系应用相空间表示，通过积分掉环境自由度，导出仅针对系统S的包含记忆核和环境噪声贡献的广义朗之万方程；采用正规序表示（如P表示及其正定变体）。

Result: 得到了一个非马尔可夫朗之万框架，其中非马尔可夫效应仅出现在具有记忆核的确定性项中，且当环境初始处于真空态时噪声项消失；成功应用于延迟反馈激光问题，重现了阈值以上的线宽压窄现象并预测了阈值附近的增强压窄。

Conclusion: 该框架使相空间方法可系统地应用于非马尔可夫驱动耗散量子系统，且随机变量数量随系统规模线性增长，适合从小系统到多体系统的广泛问题。

Abstract: Phase-space methods are powerful tools for the treatment of Markovian open quantum systems: they map the reduced dynamics of a system S, in interaction with an environment E, exactly onto Langevin equations for c-number stochastic variables, as opposed to Heisenberg-Langevin equations for operators. Langevin equations provide analytical insight in key regimes and excel at handling strong nonlinearities and couplings, where other methods often falter. Extending phase-space methods to non-Markovian dynamics, however, has remained a long-standing challenge. Here we address this gap by applying phase-space representations to the full S+E system; integrating out the environmental degrees of freedom then yields a general Langevin framework for S that incorporates both deterministic and stochastic contributions from E. Normally ordered representations, such as the Glauber-Sudarshan P representation and its positive variant due to Drummond and Gardiner, lead to Langevin equations in which (i) non-Markovian effects emerge exclusively in the deterministic terms, via a memory kernel, and (ii) noise contributions vanish when E is initially in the vacuum state. To demonstrate the power of this framework, we address the paradigmatic problem of delayed coherent feedback, in which the system is driven by its own past state, and study its impact on the laser linewidth: we recover the narrowing observed well above threshold and predict an enhanced narrowing just above it. Crucially, the number of stochastic variables scales linearly with the system size, making the framework suitable for problems ranging from a few degrees of freedom to genuinely many-body systems. This opens the way to the systematic study of non-Markovian driven-dissipative quantum systems using the same analytical and numerical tools that have long made phase-space methods so successful in the Markovian regime.

</details>


### [136] [Sufficient positive maps between von Neumann algebras: Rényi divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28510)
*Anna Jenčová,Lauritz van Luijk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在冯诺依曼代数之间正规保单位正映射下的α-z Rényi散度的恢复定理，改进了以往需要2-正性的条件，仅需正性即可。方法基于JW*-子代数及其L^p空间的充分性来刻画数据处理不等式中等号成立的条件，并解决了Haagerup和Stormer提出的一个关于条件期望分解的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进已有结果中对映射正性条件的要求（从2-正性降至单纯正性），并解决冯诺依曼代数中关于条件期望因子分解的开放问题。

Method: 利用JW*-子代数及其L^p空间的充分性理论，分析数据处理不等式中等号成立的条件，进而证明恢复定理并应用于条件期望的结构问题。

Result: 1. 在仅假设正性的条件下证明了α-z Rényi散度的恢复定理；2. 解决了Haagerup与Stormer提出的条件期望分解问题：任意向JW*-子代数的条件期望可分解为先映射到其所生成的冯诺依曼子代数的条件期望。

Conclusion: 正性足以保证α-z Rényi散度的恢复性质，无需更强的2-正性假设；同时揭示了JW*-子代数上条件期望的深层结构。

Abstract: We prove recovery theorems for $α$-$z$ Rényi divergences under normal unital positive maps between von Neumann algebras. Previous results in this setting required 2-positivity, while recent finite dimensional work showed that this assumption can be relaxed to mere positivity. Our proof uses sufficiency for JW*-subalgebras and $L^p$-spaces over them to characterize equality in the data processing inequality. As a further application of our methods, we solve a problem discussed by Haagerup and Stormer: Every conditional expectation of a von Neumann algebra onto a JW*-subalgebra factors through a conditional expectation onto the generated von Neumann subalgebra.

</details>


### [137] [Hardware-Efficient Error Mitigation and Shot-Efficient Sampling on IBM Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28535)
*Sumit Chongder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究在有限执行预算下，结合多种误差缓解技术，在超导量子硬件上系统评估了采样与误差缓解的权衡，提供了可重现的实验依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在当前含噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）设备上，如何在有限资源下有效提升量子计算结果的准确性是一个关键挑战。现有研究常假设无限采样，缺乏对实际执行预算约束下的综合评估。

Method: 采用校准感知的量子比特选择、电路深度缩放、零噪声外推、动态解耦、读出误差缓解和重复采样估计等多种技术，结合理想模拟、噪声模拟和真实设备实验，分离采样不确定性和设备误差，并在固定执行预算下评估不同策略。

Result: 量化了电路深度、噪声强度、量子比特连接性和测量预算对缓解效果的影响，发现有限采样波动可能抵消额外缓解开销带来的收益，明确了误差缓解在何种条件下能改善期望值估计。

Conclusion: 误差缓解的有效性高度依赖于硬件条件和资源分配策略；在实际应用中需权衡缓解开销与采样统计波动，本文为当前量子处理器上的误差缓解研究提供了可复现的硬件感知分析框架。

Abstract: We experimentally study error mitigation and finite-shot sampling on superconducting quantum hardware under a constrained execution budget. The study combines calibration-aware qubit selection, circuit-depth scaling, zero-noise extrapolation, dynamical decoupling, readout-error mitigation, and repeated-shot estimation on an IBM Quantum processor. Experiments are organized across ideal simulation, noise-model simulation, and physical-device execution to separate sampling uncertainty from device-induced error. We investigate how mitigation performance changes with circuit depth, effective noise scale, qubit connectivity, and measurement budget, and quantify accuracy using expectation-value error, mean-squared error, statistical uncertainty, and mitigation gain. A fixed hardware-execution budget is used to evaluate shot allocation strategies and repeated measurements without relying on unlimited sampling. The resulting analysis provides a hardware-aware characterization of when mitigation improves expectation-value estimation and when finite-shot fluctuations offset the benefit of additional mitigation overhead. The implementation uses contemporary Qiskit and IBM Quantum Runtime workflows and is designed to provide reproducible experimental evidence for error-mitigation studies on current quantum processors.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum Fourier transform for the symmetric group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28569)
*Carli Bruinsma,Dmitry Grinko,Maris Ozols*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了对称群量子傅里叶变换的构造，修正了先前算法的门复杂度和电路深度，并提出了更简化的实现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期研究已认识到非阿贝尔群的量子傅里叶变换的重要性，但已有构造在复杂度和实现上仍有改进空间。

Method: 通过对Kawano和Sekigawa算法的仔细分析，重新评估其复杂度，并优化了横向元素的选择。

Result: 将门复杂度修正为$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^{3.5})$，电路深度修正为$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^3)$，并提出更简单的实现方案。

Conclusion: 本文改进了对称群QFT的构造，降低了实现难度，为进一步应用提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Quantum Fourier transforms (QFT) for general groups were recognized to be fundamental already early in the field. A canonical example of non-abelian QFT for the symmetric group was outlined by Beals (1997). Later, a more detailed analysis of this algorithm was carried out by Kawano and Sekigawa (2016). In this paper, we revisit that construction. After a careful analysis, we revise their gate complexity to $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^{3.5})$ and circuit depth to $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^3)$. Moreover, we observe that their construction is not optimal in the choice of transversal elements, so we propose simpler realization of the symmetric group QFT.

</details>


### [139] [Learning to Decode Concatenated Quantum Codes with Hierarchical Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28571)
*Jiahui Wu,Chao Zhang,Zipeng Wu,Shilin Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于神经消息传递的通用级联稳定子码解码框架，通过双向传播软信念并使用轻量级神经网络聚合消息，在多种噪声模型下显著提升了阈值和逻辑门失败率性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的级联稳定子码解码器在处理复杂噪声和电路级误差时性能有限，尤其依赖硬判决和组合搜索，难以高效扩展到非CSS或深层级联结构。

Method: 设计了一个神经消息传递框架，使软信念在级联层级间双向传播，并利用轻量级神经网络学习消息聚合方式，从而实现对一般级联稳定子码的高效解码。

Result: 在级联[[15,7,3]]量子汉明码上，新解码器在比特翻转和去极化噪声下的阈值显著高于现有最先进解码器，去极化伪阈值从6.5%提升至12.3%；对于多超立方体码，在Knill纠错协议下逻辑CNOT失败率更低，且无需复杂搜索。

Conclusion: 该框架为探索级联码的设计空间提供了一个通用解码工具，支持包括非CSS码在内的低开销容错量子计算方案。

Abstract: We introduce a neural message-passing framework for decoding general concatenated stabilizer codes. Soft beliefs propagate bidirectionally across concatenation levels, and lightweight neural networks learn only to aggregate incoming messages. For the concatenated $[[15,7,3]]$ quantum Hamming code, the resulting decoder achieves substantially higher thresholds than the state-of-the-art bidirectional hard-decision decoder under both bit-flip and depolarizing noise. In particular, the depolarizing pseudo-threshold nearly doubles, from $6.5\%$ to $12.3\%$. For many-hypercube codes, a decoder fine-tuned on circuit-level errors in Knill's teleportation-based error correction can achieve lower logical-CNOT failure rates than their dedicated decoder, using a fixed number of message-passing iterations instead of extensive combinatorial search. Our framework provides a generic decoding tool for exploring the design space of concatenated codes, including non-CSS constructions, toward low-overhead fault tolerance.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum Fourier transform toolbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28573)
*Carli Bruinsma,Pietro M. Posta,Joppe Stokvis,Dmitry Grinko,Maris Ozols*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种新的量子傅里叶变换（QFT）电路构造方法，分别基于Mackey理论和Clifford理论，显著降低了特定非阿贝尔群族的电路成本，并为广泛有限群类提供了系统性构造工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔群的高效QFT电路构造目前仅在少数群族中实现，本文旨在扩展可高效构造QFT的群类并降低电路复杂度。

Method: 基于Mackey理论和Clifford理论提出两种新的QFT电路构造方法，并应用于GL₂(F_q)和F≀Sₙ等群结构。

Result: 对GL₂(F_q)实现了电路规模从q的多项式级降至log q的多项式级；对wreath产品F≀Sₙ去除了|F|=poly(n)的限制，可在F具有高效QFT时实现指数级改进。

Conclusion: 新方法为更广泛的非阿贝尔群提供了系统性的高效QFT构造途径，显著提升了量子算法中QFT的应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum Fourier transforms (QFTs) are essential primitives in quantum algorithms. While abelian groups admit efficient QFT circuits, with circuit size polynomial in the logarithm of the group order, efficient constructions are known for relatively few non-abelian families. We develop two new approaches to QFT circuit construction, based on Mackey theory and Clifford theory, respectively, and use them to show exponential improvement in circuit cost for specific group families. Using the Mackey-theoretic approach, we obtain explicit quantum circuits for the QFT over $\mathrm{GL}_2(F_q)$ that scale polynomially in $\log q$, rather than polynomially in $q$. Using the Clifford-theoretic approach, we obtain QFT circuits for wreath products $F\wr S_n$, whose cost depends on the cost of a QFT over $F$ and the size of its representation registers. This removes the restriction $|F|=\operatorname{poly}(n)$ required by previous generic constructions and can yield exponential improvements when $F$ itself has an efficient QFT. Together, these methods provide new systematic tools to construct QFTs for broad classes of finite groups.

</details>


### [141] [How Long-Range Tails Reshape Non-Hermitian Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28577)
*Ding Gu,Zhanpeng Fu,Yu-Min Hu,Zhong Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在非厄米系统中，即使是非常微小的长程跃迁也可能非微扰地重构短程系统的能谱和本征态，这种效应源于长程跃迁衰减长度与非厄米趋肤模局域长度之间的竞争，并导致一维下广义布里渊区压缩及高维下的压缩阿梅巴结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧束缚模型中指数衰减的长程跃迁在非厄米系统中被截断近似处理时可能引发的严重误差，揭示其对开边界条件下能谱和本征态的非微扰影响。

Method: 通过分析一维和高维非厄米系统中的广义布里渊区（GBZ）变化，提出‘压缩GBZ’和‘压缩阿梅巴’理论框架来描述由无穷小长程跃迁引起的能谱重构现象，并探讨其对格林函数的影响。

Result: 发现极小的长程跃迁可在开边界条件下完全重构非厄米系统的能谱与本征态；提出一维压缩GBZ和高维压缩阿梅巴模型解释该现象，并证明长程跃迁可显著改变格林函数，具有实验可观测性。

Conclusion: 传统的截断长程跃迁方法在非厄米系统中可能彻底失效，必须考虑长程项的非微扰效应，所提出的压缩GBZ和压缩阿梅巴为理解非厄米系统中长程相互作用提供了新理论工具。

Abstract: Exponentially decaying long-range hoppings are ubiquitous in realistic tight-binding models and are often truncated to obtain a finite-range description. We show that this approximation can fail dramatically in non-Hermitian systems under open boundary conditions: an infinitesimal long-range hopping can nonperturbatively reconstruct the spectrum and eigenstates of a short-range non-Hermitian system. The mechanism is controlled by a competition between the decay length of infinitesimal long-range hoppings and the localization length of non-Hermitian skin modes, leading to a sharp transition as the decay rate is tuned. In one dimension, we show that a squeezed generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) replaces the original GBZ of the short-ranged Hamiltonian, yielding the reconstructed open-boundary spectrum. In two or higher dimensions, we formulate a squeezed amoeba formulation describing the reconstructed spectral density. We further show that long-range hoppings can qualitatively reshape Green's function, which can be readily detected in experiments.

</details>


<div id='physics.ao-ph'></div>

# physics.ao-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [A Moisture-Vorticity Theory for the Boreal Summer Quasi-Biweekly Oscillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27665)
*Shubhrangshu Biswas,Jai Sukhatme,Bishakhdatta Gayen*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于湿涡度理论的热带夏季准双周振荡（QBWO）动力学解释，强调背景水汽梯度与罗斯比波动力学的耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解热带夏季准双周振荡（QBWO）的动力-对流耦合机制及其区域差异性。

Method: 在弱温度梯度慢流形框架下构建湿涡度理论模型，结合GPT-5.5辅助分析，研究水汽异常与旋转环流之间的耦合动力学。

Result: 发现QBWO本质上是多普勒频移的罗斯比模态，其增长依赖于相位一致的水汽提取和涡旋拉伸；在孟加拉湾不稳定而在中西非干燥区衰减，且存在强耦合下的湿涡极限态。

Conclusion: QBWO是一种依赖于背景水汽梯度几何结构、耦合强度、阻尼和平均流动的罗斯比-湿-涡度不稳定性现象。

Abstract: We develop a moisture vorticity theory for the boreal summer quasi-biweekly oscillation (QBWO) in the tropics and analyze it with the aid of GPT-5.5. The model is formulated on a weak-temperature-gradient slow manifold, in which moisture anomalies diagnose the divergent circulation while the rotational circulation evolves through Rossby wave dynamics and vortex stretching. The key coupling is controlled by eddy advection of the background moisture gradient, with the projection $(\mathbf{k}\cdot\nabla\bar q)$ modifying propagation and the cross-gradient factor $(\mathbf{k}\times\nabla\bar q)_z$ controlling moisture extraction and growth. In the weak-coupling regime, which is relevant for the real-world situation, the QBWO is a Doppler-shifted Rossby mode whose growth or decay is determined by phase-coherent moisture extraction and subsequent vortex stretching; moisture damping weakens this feedback but does not introduce a sharp threshold in the minimal linear model. In terms of subtropical geographical locations, this renders the QBWO unstable over the Bay of Bengal with an intraseasonal growth rate and co-located vorticity and moisture anomalies, and it decays over relatively drier regions such as Central and West Africa with a quadrature relation between vorticity and moisture anomalies. The equations also admit a strong-coupling regime where the system approaches a moist-vortex limit. The theory interprets the QBWO as a Rossby-moisture-vorticity instability whose regional behavior depends on background moisture-gradient geometry, coupling strength, damping, and mean-flow advection.

</details>


### [143] [Application of the latent twins approach for clear sky retrieval from IASI observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27692)
*Michele Martinazzo,Cristina Sgattoni,Marco Menarini,Chiara Zugarini,Tiziano Maestri,Luca Sgheri*

Main category: physics.ao-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于潜在孪生方法的新型深度学习架构，用于从IASI光谱中反演大气参数，在保证模型复杂度、数据量和训练成本平衡的同时，提供数据质量的定量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动模型在样本外泛化能力差，而物理信息神经网络计算成本高，因此需要一种兼顾泛化性、物理一致性和计算效率的新方法。

Method: 采用基于潜在空间孪生结构的深度学习架构，结合物理约束与数据驱动学习，先在NWP SAF数据库生成的合成辐射数据上验证，再应用于IASI L1C观测数据，并以L2产品作为参考进行评估。

Result: 该方法在真实晴空条件下能有效反演温度、水汽、臭氧、地表发射率和地表温度等参数，且具有较低的训练成本和良好的鲁棒性，同时可提供热力学廓线重建的误差分析策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构在保持物理一致性的同时降低了计算开销，提升了外推能力，适用于实际大气廓线反演任务，并为数据质量评估提供了量化手段。

Abstract: In recent years, data-driven approaches emerged as alternatives to traditional physics-based retrievals, taking advantage of machine learning techniques such as learnable pseudoinverse, random forests, or deep learning architectures. Classical data-driven models generalize poorly to out-of-sample regimes, as they optimize over finite datasets without incorporating underlying physical laws. This often requires large models and extensive data to achieve reliability. Physics-Informed Neural Networks address this by embedding physical constraints into the learning process, enabling improved extrapolation. However, they requires substantial computational cost due to the need to solve governing equations at each training step. In this work, we introduce a novel deep learning architecture, based on latent twin approach, that balances model complexity, dataset size, and training cost, while providing a quantitative measure of data quality. This architecture is applied to IASI spectra, with the goal to assess the robustness of this method for retrieving atmospheric profiles, including temperature, water vapor, ozone, surface emissivity, and surface temperature, in real-world clear-sky conditions. The algorithm is first applied on synthetic radiances derived from the NWP SAF database using the fast radiative transfer code sigma-IASI/F2N. After validating the architecture on synthetic data, the algorithm is applied to IASI Level 1C observations, along with their corresponding Level 2 products which serve as reference to evaluate the reconstruction accuracy of the autoencoder-based retrieval. The retrieval performances are discussed along with possible strategies to provide an error analysis for the reconstructed thermodynamical profiles.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [The Mechanics of Democratic Dominance: A System Dynamics Paradigm for Dynamic Consent Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27509)
*Muhammad Sukri Bin Ramli*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了动态同意工程（DCE）框架，将伯奈斯的公共关系理论与系统动力学的S-E-E-D模型结合，通过四维资源矩阵和非线性反馈机制，形式化分析政治主导权的构建与稳定，强调政策执行与动态反馈同步对长期政治稳定的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统政治传播框架基于线性、事件驱动的假设，无法有效解释复杂的动态政治现象，因此需要一种新的跨学科范式来揭示政治影响背后的系统性机制。

Method: 整合伯奈斯的公共关系原则与S-E-E-D系统动力学框架，构建四维资源矩阵（人力、智力、资本与算法媒体基础设施），并将伯奈斯八步工作流映射到非线性反馈回路、信息时滞和人口承载能力上，建立动态模型。

Result: 成功识别出政治弹性、政策阻力平台期和阈值触发的信任崩溃等现象的系统根源，并提出一个四步诊断流程，通过历史参照模式验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 长期政治稳定不依赖于短暂的修辞，而取决于政策执行与动态反馈调节之间的结构性同步，DCE为理解和优化民主制度中的政治主导提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Traditional frameworks of political communication operate under linear, event-driven assumptions that treat voter persuasion as a static, transactional function. This paper introduces Dynamic Consent Engineering (DCE), a novel interdisciplinary paradigm that synthesizes Edward Bernays' foundational principles of public relations with the S-E-E-D (Snowball, Equilibrium, Elasticity, Dominance) framework of system dynamics. By expanding Bernaysian operational constraints into a four-dimensional resource matrix (incorporating algorithmic media infrastructure alongside manpower, mindpower, and capital), we mathematically formalize how democratic institutions construct, optimize, and sustain political dominance. We map Bernays' classic eight-step engineering workflow directly onto non-linear feedback loops, information time lags, and demographic carrying capacities. Through rigorous modeling of systemic feedback structures, we isolate the dynamic root causes of political elasticity, policy resistance plateaus, and threshold-triggered trust collapses. Finally, we establish a four-step diagnostic pipeline and validate the framework via historical reference mode verification, demonstrating that long-term political stability depends not on ephemeral rhetoric, but on the structural synchronization of policy execution and dynamic feedback calibration.

</details>


### [145] [Criticality and universality in network dismantling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27613)
*Lorenzo Cirigliano,Claudio Castellano,Minsuk Kim,Filippo Radicchi,Hanlin Sun*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应偏置渗流过程来研究网络瓦解的物理机制，发现该过程在不同网络中表现出普适的相变特性，表明网络瓦解的关键行为可能具有拓扑无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示网络瓦解过程中网络结构连通性的变化机制，特别是在热力学极限下的物理规律。

Method: 引入一种自适应偏置渗流过程，并在合成网络模型和真实网络上进行系统分析与模拟。

Result: 发现所提出的渗流过程在不同度分布的网络中均表现出普适的相变，巨连通分支和最大2核同时突变消失；真实网络的模拟结果支持这一普适性。

Conclusion: 网络瓦解的物理机制对多种拓扑性质不敏感，暗示可能存在一种与拓扑无关的理论来解释其临界行为。

Abstract: Identifying the smallest set of elements whose removal dismantle a complex network, known as the network dismantling problem, is a fundamental task with many practical applications. Whereas network dismantling has been extensively studied over the past decade, most work has focused on developing efficient algorithms for large but finite networks. By contrast, the physics of the network dismantling process, namely how the network structural connectivity is affected by the removal of nodes or edges, remains largely unexplored in the thermodynamic limit. Here, we shed light on this understudied aspect of network dismantling by introducing an adaptive biased percolation process able to optimally dismantle a network. Through a systematic analysis of synthetic network models, we find that the proposed percolation process displays a universal phase transition, characterized by the abrupt and simultaneous disappearance of both the giant connected component and the largest 2-core, across networks with markedly different degree distributions. Simulations on real networks further support this universality, indicating that the physics of network dismantling is insensitive to a broad range of topological properties. Together, these results suggest that a topology-agnostic theory could be developed to explain the critical behavior of network dismantling.

</details>


### [146] [Don't look now: paying for documented need punishes discovery in US lead-pipe funding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27736)
*Gustavo Pedro Ricou,Kristin C. Epstein*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究分析了美国一项基于环境指标的铅服务管道更换资金分配规则，发现当前规则会惩罚那些积极检测铅管的州，导致资金分配不公；作者提出了一种基于审计的、防策略操纵的修正公式，能够消除对检测行为的惩罚，大幅减少资金分配波动，并证明该修正方案在仅需花费1.36%年度资金进行审计的情况下即可实现更公平且有效的分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于自我报告的铅管数据的资金分配规则可能导致州政府因发现更多铅管而遭受资金减少，从而抑制其检测积极性，造成激励扭曲。研究旨在揭示这一问题并提出一种不会惩罚需求测量的公平分配机制。

Method: 通过重构现行资金分配规则，评估其对各州预期资金的影响；证明任何固定公式若依赖自我报告的库存数据，则无法避免惩罚需求测量；推导出一种唯一的加性修正方法——基于审计的防策略操纵估计器，并使用该项目的数据进行校准和验证。

Result: 现行规则导致在8个可衡量的司法管辖区中有3个在发现未知管道后预期资金减少，无一增加；系统不提交数据则被记为无铅，形成负面激励；修正后的公式将发现铅管的中位成本从每年每根线48.63美元降至零，资金分配波动从16.5%下降至0.8%，仅需投入1.36%年度资金用于审计。

Conclusion: 基于自我报告数据的固定资金分配公式本质上会惩罚对需求的准确测量；唯有引入审计锚定的机制才能实现公正、激励相容的资源分配，同时保留对弱势系统的倾斜支持。

Abstract: The United States is allotting USD 15 billion for lead service line replacement in proportion to documented lead, the largest of a growing class of environmental-indicator transfers. Reconstructing the rule exactly, we show it prices looking below zero. Resolving an unknown pipe lowers a state's expected allotment in three of the eight jurisdictions we can measure above the statutory floor, and raises it in none. Below it the allotment cannot move. Systems that file nothing are scored lead-free. We prove no fixed formula on self-reported inventories can pay for need without punishing its measurement, derive the unique additive repair -- an audit-anchored, strategy-proof estimator -- and calibrate it on the program's data. Discovery is repriced from a median loss of USD 48.63 per line per year to zero. Allocation swings fall from 16.5% of the pool to 0.8%, for an audit costing 1.36% of one year's funds. The formula's tilt toward vulnerable systems survives.

</details>


### [147] [China's Shrinking Home Bias and Rising Disruptive Impact: Evidence from a Global Citation Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28139)
*Nan Deng,Zheng Ma,Xiaohui Liu*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 中国科研论文的国内引用偏好被高估，过去二十年该倾向持续下降，且中国论文在产生颠覆性成果方面正逐步接近美国水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨中国科学出版物增长是否因过度的国内引用而被夸大，并评估中国研究的实际国际影响力。

Method: 基于Web of Science（1980-2025）超过4500万篇出版物的引用网络，使用网络重排零模型控制发表量的结构效应，分析中国的本土引用偏差和研究影响力。

Result: 中国的本土引用偏差不如普遍认为的严重，且在过去二十年稳步下降；中国研究人员的本土引用偏好并不显著强于其他主要国家；中国论文在引发范式转变方面的潜力正逐渐向美国论文靠拢。

Conclusion: 中国科学研究的发展并未受困于封闭或自引膨胀，反而显示出日益增强的国际化趋势和不断提升的研究影响力，挑战了关于中国科研存在严重本土偏见的主流观点。

Abstract: China has become the world's largest producer of scientific publications, yet concerns persist that this growth is inflated by excessive domestic citation practices. In this study, we analyze a citation network of over 45 million publications from Web of Science (1980-2025) to investigate China's home citation bias and research impact. Using a network reshuffling null model to control for the structural effect of publication volume, we find that China's home citation bias is less pronounced than commonly assumed and has been steadily declining over the past two decades. Chinese researchers do not exhibit a significantly stronger home citation preference than other major countries, indicating increasing internationalization rather than insularity. Furthermore, using the persistent disruption framework, we show that Chinese papers are converging toward American papers in their capacity to produce paradigm-shifting work. These findings challenge prevailing narratives about Chinese scientific home bias and suggest that China's advances in research impact.

</details>


### [148] [Benchmarking large language model agent societies against human behavioural distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28182)
*Raad Bin Tareaf*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SILICA，一个用于评估大语言模型代理在模拟人类社会行为时可靠性的开源工具。实验表明，当前的模型仅在初始行为上与人类数据一致，但在长期合作、策略调整和激励响应方面表现不佳，且结果对实验设置敏感，说明现有‘硅基社会’仅适用于探索性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 怀疑大语言模型代理是否能真实反映人类行为、其研究发现是否对实验设计变化稳健，以及观察到的社会动态是否仅为模型记忆的再现而非真实互动。

Method: 开发了SILICA工具，包含五个具有人类基准数据的环境，每个环境都配有规则不变但形式扰动的变体，以及偏离记忆化结果的收益结构。在单个消费级显卡上运行12个开源权重模型进行测试。

Result: 只有8/11个模型在第一轮公共物品贡献上接近人类数据，无一模型匹配最终合作水平或人类合作区间；动作顺序调换导致某模型合作度下降58点；仅1个经过推理训练的模型能正确设定接受阈值；约定形成依赖名称先验而非协商，先验破坏后协商才出现。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型代理社会仅能在起点上模拟人类行为，缺乏稳健性和真实交互机制，因此只适合支持探索性主张，不能作为可靠的社会实验平台。

Abstract: Populations of large language model agents are increasingly used as experimental societies. Three doubts shadow every such result: whether the agents behave like the humans they stand in for, whether a finding survives changes to the apparatus that leave the rules untouched, and whether apparent social dynamics are interaction at all rather than the reproduction of experiments the models have read. This article introduces SILICA, an open instrument that tests all three. Five environments carry published human anchors, each paired with perturbations that re-render the same rules and with variants whose payoffs point away from the memorised result. Twelve open-weight models were run through it on a single consumer graphics card. Agreement with human data is confined to starting points: first-round public-goods contributions fall inside the equivalence margin for eight of eleven models, while no model matches end-state contributions or the human corridor of cooperation. Merely swapping the order in which two actions are listed costs one model 58 points of cooperation. Presenting responders with a fixed schedule of offers shows that only one model, the sole reasoning-trained one, places its acceptance threshold where the incentive requires; two move theirs part of the way, two move them the wrong way, and three never acquire one. Conventions form through a shared prior over the names rather than through negotiation, though negotiation reappears once that prior is disrupted. On the certification ladder defined here, current silicon societies support exploratory claims and no more.

</details>


### [149] [Early warning signals for synchronization transitions from partial observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28320)
*Yusuke Kato,Naoki Masuda*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 研究了在部分观测条件下，如何通过少量哨兵节点检测网络中集体同步的早期预警信号，发现基于局部序参量和去趋势相位方差的指标结合动态选择的哨兵节点能有效预测同步转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于在实际网络系统中难以观测所有振子，因此需要探索仅通过少量监测节点来提前预警集体同步的发生。

Method: 采用随机Kuramoto模型，数值比较三种早期预警信号（局部序参量、其时间方差、去趋势相位方差）以及不同哨兵节点选择策略（基于动力学、度数或随机选择）。

Result: 局部序参量和去趋势相位方差比局部序参量的时间方差提供更强的预警能力；基于节点动力学行为选择哨兵节点表现最优；仅需⌊ln N⌋个动态选定的哨兵即可接近全网络观测的效果。

Conclusion: 在稀疏观测下，通过合理选择预警信号和哨兵节点，可以有效预测同步转变。

Abstract: Anticipating the onset of collective synchronization is important in many networked systems, yet observing every oscillator is often impractical. We investigate whether synchronization transitions can be detected from a small set of monitored, or sentinel, nodes. Using a stochastic Kuramoto model on networks, we numerically compare three early warning signals: the local order parameter, its temporal variance, and the variance of individual oscillator phases after removing their mean rotational trends. We also compare sentinel-selection strategies based on node dynamics, degree, and random sampling. We show that, under partial observation, the local order parameter and the variance of detrended phases provide substantially stronger warning signals than the variance of the local order parameter. Selecting nodes according to their dynamical behavior near the transition consistently improves performance over other sentinel-selection methods. With only $\lfloor\ln N\rfloor$ dynamically selected sentinels, two success indicators approach the performance obtained by observing all $N$ nodes. These results demonstrate that synchronization transitions can be anticipated from sparse observations when the warning signal and monitored nodes are chosen appropriately.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Community corrections have divergent downstream effects across corrected accounts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27526)
*Yuwei Chuai,Thomas Renault,Nicolas Pröllochs,Gabriele Lenzini,Mohsen Mosleh*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 社区事实核查可以减少被标记的误导性帖子的传播，但对发布者的长期行为影响有限，尤其对于反复发布误导信息的用户效果不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究社区事实核查机制（如X平台的Community Notes）是否能对被纠正用户产生持久的行为改变。

Method: 基于X平台上的Community Notes系统进行大规模准实验研究，采用双重差分法（Difference-in-Differences），追踪19,854个账号在标注前后四周的行为变化，涵盖57,935次纠正事件和1190万条原始帖子。

Result: 整体上，标注后被纠正账号的发帖量平均增加2.9%。其中，仅被纠正一次的账号发帖量减少2.4%，发布内容毒性与误导性下降；而占28.6%但贡献73.4%被查证帖子的反复被纠正账号，在首次纠正后发帖量增加4.4%，后续纠正无显著反应，且内容更误导、引用来源质量更低、政治内容更多。

Conclusion: 社区事实核查能有效抑制单个误导性帖子的传播，但难以持续改善最主要误导内容生产者的行为，表明‘纠正内容’与‘改变发布者行为’是平台设计中两个不同的目标。

Abstract: Community-based fact-checking can reduce the spread of annotated misleading posts, but whether it produces lasting behavioral change among corrected authors remains unclear. Here, we conduct a large-scale quasi-experimental study of the Community Notes system on X (formerly Twitter), tracking four weeks of activity before and after note display for 19,854 accounts and 57,935 corrections (noted posts and matched controls), covering 11,909,591 original posts. Difference-in-Differences estimates show that note display is followed by an average 2.9% increase in corrected accounts' original-post activity. This aggregate conceals two divergent trajectories. Accounts corrected only once reduce their activity by 2.4% and subsequently publish less toxic and less misleading content. Repeatedly corrected accounts, which constitute 28.6% of corrected accounts but produce 73.4% of fact-checked posts, instead increase their activity by 4.4% after their first correction and show no detectable response to later ones. They exhibit no comparable content improvement, and instead publish more highly misleading posts, cite lower-quality domains, and post more political content. Community notes can thus constrain individual misleading posts without durably improving the behavior of the accounts most responsible for them, indicating that correcting content and changing its producers are distinct objectives for platform design.

</details>


### [151] [Why People Share Social Media Screenshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27539)
*Tarannum Zaki,Michael L. Nelson,Michele C. Weigle*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了用户在社交媒体平台上分享社交媒体帖子截图的动机，分析了这种行为如何影响信息的传播与解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解用户为何分享社交媒体截图，尤其是这些行为在增强平台功能和跨平台互操作性的同时，也可能导致信息脱离上下文、来源被隐藏或误导受众。

Method: 通过聚焦于用户在Twitter/X、Instagram和Facebook等平台上的实际行为，分析其分享截图背后的动机。

Result: 揭示了截图分享既具有正当用途（如提升表达控制权），也存在潜在风险（如断章取义、误导公众）。

Conclusion: 截图分享是一种双刃剑，既能增强用户表达能力，也可能扭曲信息传播，需进一步规范以减少误用。

Abstract: Posting screenshots of social media posts is a common way of increasing platform functionality, enabling inter-platform interoperability, and gives users more control over how content is presented and interpreted. While these are legitimate purposes, screenshot sharing is also used in ways that can remove context, hide the original source, or mislead audiences. We examine the motivations behind sharing screenshots of social media posts on social media platforms like Twitter/X, Instagram, and Facebook. By focusing on user motivations, our study contributes to understanding how screenshot practices shape the sharing and interpretation of information in online spaces.

</details>


### [152] [Scalable dynamic community detection on temporal graphs using graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28342)
*Peijie Zhong,Raul Mondragon,Richard G. Clegg*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散引导对比学习的动态社区检测框架，通过构建局部时间扩散亲和矩阵来组织节点-时间实例的表示，并在合成和真实时序网络上验证了其有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态社区检测方法难以同时捕捉节点成员关系的动态变化和时间结构特征，需要一种能够对节点-时间实例进行细粒度建模的方法。

Method: 设计了一个扩散引导的对比学习框架，利用局部时间扩散亲和矩阵构建正负样本对，学习节点-时间实例的表示，再在嵌入空间中应用聚类算法检测动态社区。

Result: 在合成时序网络上的实验表明，该方法在AMI和ARI指标上优于静态基线并达到或超过了现有动态方法的表现，且具有良好可扩展性；在OpenAlex科研合作网络上的应用揭示了真实场景中持续演化的关系社区。

Conclusion: 基于节点-时间实例的表示学习为时序图上的动态社区检测提供了一个有效的可扩展框架，扩散引导的对比学习能更好地捕捉时间与结构的联合特征。

Abstract: Dynamic community detection on temporal graphs seeks to identify evolving community structures while allowing node memberships to change over time. In this work, we formulate dynamic community detection over observed node-time instances, where each node-time instance in the temporal interaction stream is assigned a cluster label. We propose a diffusion-guided contrastive learning framework that uses a local temporal diffusion affinity matrix to construct positive and negative node-time pairs and organise the learned representations according to their temporal structural relationships. We then apply a clustering algorithm to the resulting embedding space to detect dynamic communities. Experiments on synthetic temporal networks show that the proposed method outperforms static community detection baselines and achieves competitive or better performance than existing dynamic community detection methods in terms of AMI and ARI, while maintaining good scalability. We further apply the method to a large-scale OpenAlex computer science collaboration network from 2016 to 2025, revealing persistent and evolving collaboration communities in real scientific data. These results suggest that time-node-level representation learning provides an effective framework for scalable dynamic community detection on temporal graphs.

</details>


### [153] [Structural Change and Random Graph Models in Global Oil Trade Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28474)
*Anthony Bonato,Vincent Luong,Kyne Santos*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究利用网络方法分析了全球石油贸易的结构变化，发现基于度分布的Chung-Lu模型最能反映其子图特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全球石油贸易网络的演化及其结构特征，揭示关键时间节点和驱动因素。

Method: 采用联合国商品贸易数据库，结合加权入度、PageRank、Louvain社区检测和node2vec嵌入等网络分析方法，并通过3-和4-节点子图谱及机器学习分类比较随机图模型。

Result: 识别出1991年、2011年、2017年和2021年的重大结构变化；模块化程度从1990年代到2010年代下降；油贸网络始终被分类为Chung-Lu图，而非几何模型。

Conclusion: 全球石油贸易网络的结构演化受国家间连接模式的度分布主导，地理集聚性减弱，Chung-Lu模型最能刻画其拓扑特征。

Abstract: We studied structural change in global oil trade using a network approach. Using UN Comtrade data, we examined the temporal evolution of international trade networks, with an emphasis on crude oil. Weighted in-degree identified major changes in country rankings in 1991, 2011, 2017, and 2021, while PageRank detected pronounced changes around 1991 and 2024. The Louvain algorithm identified clear geographic communities within the overall trade network. In the oil trade network, modularity declined from the 1990s to the 2010s, with node2vec embeddings showing weaker clustering in 2011 than in 1991. We also compared the oil trade network with several random graph models using 3- and 4-node subgraph profiles and machine learning classification. The oil trade network was consistently classified as a Chung-Lu graph, while the Geometric model was not favored, suggesting that a model based on the degree distribution better matched its subgraph profiles than the other models considered.

</details>


### [154] [Understanding Venture Capital Syndication in Information Technology Sectors: A Network Formation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28526)
*Liheng Tan,Zhengkai Tu,Prasanna Karhade*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 该研究利用1966至2024年美国信息技术领域的PitchBook数据，分析风险投资联合投资网络的形成机制，发现 prior collaboration、共享合作伙伴、地理邻近和组织类型相似性是联合投资关系建立的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解风险投资联合投资网络的结构形成机制，揭示关系持续性、网络闭合性和同质性如何共同塑造投资网络。

Method: 使用全样本二元logit模型（带聚类标准误）分析硬件、软件和混合子行业的完全二元数据集，并结合2024年软件子行业1100家活跃投资者的静态指数随机图模型（ERGM）进行补充分析。

Result: 在所有子行业中，过往合作是最稳定的联合投资预测因子；共享伙伴、地理接近和组织类型相似性也显著促进联合投资；领域重叠、声望和经验的影响则因情境而异。ERGM结果显示三元闭包和地理同质性有正向影响，类型同质性影响较小但为正。

Conclusion: 关系持久性、网络闭合性和同质性共同塑造了IT风险投资联合投资网络的结构；未来工作将引入时序网络模型、市场冲击反事实模拟和基于网络的合作伙伴推荐评估。

Abstract: Venture capital syndication enables investors to pool diligence, share risk, and signal venture quality, while shaping the relationships through which investment networks develop. We examine how prior relationships, network embeddedness, and organizational similarity structure annual co-investment link formation in U.S. information technology venture finance. Using dyad-complete PitchBook panels for the hardware, software, and hybrid subsectors from 1966 to 2024, we test seven mechanisms through full-sample dyadic logit models with dyad-clustered standard errors. Across subsectors, prior collaboration is the most consistent correlate of co-investment; shared partners, geographic proximity, and organizational-type similarity are also positively associated with link formation, while domain overlap, prominence, and experience vary across settings. A static ERGM of the 2024 software network among 1,100 persistently active investors likewise produces positive estimates for triadic closure and geographic homophily and a smaller positive estimate for type homophily. By combining complete dyadic risk sets with a whole-network specification, the study shows how relational persistence, network closure, and homophily jointly structure IT venture syndication networks. In future work, we will extend the analysis with temporal network models, counterfactual simulations of market shocks, and evaluations of network-aware partner recommendations.

</details>
